LEFT JOIN (

    FROM [Rooms] AS [r]
    WHERE [r].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [r0] ON [h].[Id] = [r0].[HotelId]
WHERE [h].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
ORDER BY [h].[Id]
